Let's design improved multiplicative descent:
Function `_mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, mode_params)`:
- p normalized, floor small.
- best p/score.
- beta schedule: perhaps use a mix of softmax and hard top. For each iteration, choose active weight mode:
   - 'soft': weights softmax(d * beta) with d=m*c maybe or normalized z = c / M. beta adapt from 10 to 500.
   - 'hard': equal weights on top B constraints whose c >= theta*M (theta=0.95 or 0.9) to flatten plateau. B maybe 32 or 64.
   - 'mixed': weights ∝ softmax + uniform over top.
- Compute h as gradient h_j = valid_front(weights, p). Normalize h to max=1 or sum=1.
- Direction p_new = p * exp(-alpha h). Since h can be large at positions involved in peaks. This will reduce peaks but also may make vector spiky? We need ensure nonnegative. Normalize.
- Step size: Use line search over alphas: [base*0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2,4] maybe; accept if exact score decreases and smooth decreases. Since exact objective expensive but one FFT per alpha. We can first choose based on linearized? Use exact. For long n, 6 FFTs per iter maybe okay if 20k iters? 2400s maybe 1 iter ~0.005s? 6*0.005=0.03, ~80k iters possible in Python? Maybe less. Need not too many.
- Could use second-order scaling: alpha = step_size / (p max?)? In previous p *= exp(-a h/maxh). We can adapt alpha based on h distribution. Start alpha ~0.05? For softmax h max small? Need tune.
- Add small negative entropy regularization? Actually multiplicative update with h max1 and alpha. If alpha too large, peak values collapse to few positions and may local. We can limit alpha by KL: not too far from p. Use max log change = α max(h) ≤ maybe 1.5.
- Momentum? Could combine with previous best: p = geometric interpolation (log mix) between current and candidate? e.g., p_new = p_old^γ * p_cand^(1-γ). But normalization. This may stabilize.
- Restart / perturb: if stall, multiply random noise exp(N(0,σ)) or blend with best/uniform/chirp.

Now, enhanced MMD detailed:
```
def _mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=1500.0, alpha_max=1.0, verbose=False, every_fixed? )
```
- p normalized, floor.
- best p, best_score.
- cur score.
- alpha = 0.05? Previous alpha 0.1. Use adaptive.
- beta starts maybe 10, increases gradually. 
- It loop while time rem.
   - compute c=conv(p,p) using FFT. maxc. score=2n maxc.
   - d = n*c (so max ~1-? uniform max=1; target max=score/(2)=? For n, score=2max(d)? Wait score=2n maxc =2 max(n c). So score=2 max(d). For target 1.5, max(d)=0.75. Uniform max(d)=1. Great. So d in [0,1]. Softmax beta*d.
   - Choose active weights. We can keep a `mode` loop:
        * If iteration count % 3 !=? Use hard top and soft mix to avoid over-softmax rounding.
        * Threshold: top_mask = d >= 0.9*maxd. weights = softmax(beta*d) + small*top_mask; normalize. Or weights over top B with weights proportional to (d - maxd + epsilon). 
      Maybe use `top_b = min(128, max(8, n//64))`. For hard: weights uniform over top B constraints. But if plateau, B enough.
   - h = valid_grad(weights, p). h nonnegative. If maxh==0: perturb.
   - To ensure direction reduces current max, h at positions? If active includes center peak, h positive.
   - Normalize h such that max h =1? Or sum h=1? Previous normalized by maxh. If maxh large, alpha same log change per high h. Good.
   - Candidate alphas: based on previous alpha. Generate list e.g. [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.6]. For each: `q = p * exp(-a * h)`; normalize; floor; exact score; accept if exact < best_score and smooth? We want descent. Since line search exact uses multiple FFT (cost). Functions for generators:
- `_uniform(n)`: ones/n.
- `_two_block(n, a, ratio)`: first part 1, second ratio, normalized. We can sample grid.
- `_ramp(n, phase)`: linear up/down. But maybe not best; include.
- `_chirp(n, rng, mode)`:
   modes:
   0: `C + eps * cos(2π f i/n + φ)` with f maybe 0.5..10 (non-integer), eps≤C. Better to use `base=1`, `amp=rng.uniform(0.3,0.9)`, `freq=rng.lognormal(1,1)` (not necessarily integer), `phase`; p = base + amp*cos(...), clip min 1e-6.
   1: sum of 2-5 cosines with frequencies from {noninteger, integer} eps. Use positive part: p=1+Σ eps_r cos(2π f_r i + φ_r); clip.
   2: chirp: p=1+amp*cos(ω_i + α i^2/n + φ) clip.
   3: spline/random blocks as previous.
   4: Fourier random phasor: p = 1 + Σ_{r=1}^R eps_r cos(2π f_r i + φ_r); ensure min. We can use `np.maximum(p, 1e-6)`.
   5: periodic base repeated: generate short pattern L via random positive and repeat.
- `_fourier_blend(n, rng, base=1, ncomp=3, ampmax=0.8)`: frequencies maybe uniformly in [0.5,10], amps `ampmax * rng.random()**0.5 * exp(-idx/3)`, phase uniform. p=1+sum... Clip. This can create noninteger frequency ripples. Normalize.
- `_two_level_random(n, rng, K)`: random block levels lognormal, like previous.
- `_smooth_white(n)`: Fourier white noise positive: random spectrum with zero mean, IFFT, add 1, positive, smooth? Could be highly oscillatory. Use p=1+noise amplitude.
- `_periodic_initial(n, L, rng)`: generate p_L length L using random Fourier (maybe nonnegative) then tile/truncate to n? If n not multiple, interpolate/resample tiled.

def _sanitize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    a=a/s
    # maybe cap after normalize? if max>1000? impossible unless n small; but if cap changes sum; loop?
    if a.max()>1000: a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s>0 a=a/s
    return a
```

Maybe there is an analytical construction using "low cross-correlation binary sequences" that could directly get below 1.5. Let's explore deterministic "Gold sequences" around n=3072. Could generate m-sequence length 1023 (2^10-1) or 4095. Binary sequence with ideal periodic autocorrelation. If we take a_i = (1 + s_i)/2 with s_i ±1 m-sequence on period L, and maybe repeat/interpolate to n. What is score? Let's approximate.

Let a_i = 0.5 + 0.5 s_i (binary, s zero mean). P=0.5 all-ones +0.5 S. Square coefficients:
0.25 A^2 +0.5 A*S +0.25 S^2.
For period L repeated? The sequence length n maybe multiple L. For a period, full conv of A (all ones L) triangular peak L. S^2 coefficients are counts of equal signs? For m-sequence, autocorrelation differences ideal, but square of S uses sum i+j=k, equivalent to cross-correlation between S and reversed S. If S is m-sequence, its reverse also an m-sequence; cross-correlation of two m-sequences may be low? Perhaps. But A term baseline: 0.25 * A*A. For A length L sum L, A^2 max ~L at center? Actually A*A max L at center. S^2 coefficients? Sum of S over a period 0 (if balanced), sum S^2=L. Full conv S*S coefficients bounded maybe ~? If S random signs, S*S coefficients ~ product? Wait S*S: coefficient ∑ s_i s_{k-i}. Since s_i s_{k-i} product of signs; not correlation difference. Random signs coefficients ~√L. Max maybe few√L. A baseline max ~0.25 L. Sum a =0.5L. Score for period =2L*max / (0.5L)^2 ≈8 * max / L. Baseline max ~0.25L => score ~2. Cross can reduce baseline? baseline triangular max at center; if S^2 or A*S produce negative at center? S^2 can be negative (products negative), A*S zero mean maybe. To get score 1.5, need max ≈(1.5/8)L=0.1875L, saving 25% from baseline. Random S^2 fluctuations √L not enough for large L? Implementation details for FFT convolution:
```
def _conv_full(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b)
    if la ==0 or lb==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if la*lb <= 2000000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    nout=la+lb-1
    L=_next_pow2(nout)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return out[:nout]
```
For square p,p, call twice? Could optimize `_conv_self(p)` with one transform:
```
def _conv_self(p):
   n=len(p)
   if n*n <= 2e6: return np.convolve(p,p)
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
   return out[:2*n-1]
```
Good.

Valid gradient:
```
def _valid_grad(weights,p):
   n=len(p); W=len(weights)
   if n==0: return []
   if W ==0: return zeros n
   L=_next_pow2(max(W, 2*n))  # actually need W+n-1? But previous used 2n enough because W=2n-1 and output n; to be safe use W+n
   # circular correlation: out[j]=sum_i p[i] weights[(j+i)%L]
   # Need L >= max index j+i without wrap: j<=n-1,i<=n-1 => 2n-2. weights index up to W-1. So L >= max(W,2n-2). Use W+? 2n safe.
   fw=np.fft.rfft(weights,L)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp),L)
   return out[:n]
```
Will circular wrap if L < W? L>=W? If W=2n-1, L=2n (if 2n power) or more, okay. If n not power? We choose pow2 for p maybe not; still use W+n. For n 1024, W=2047, 2n=2048, L=2048. Max j+i=2046, okay. For n=4096, W=8191, 2n=8192, L=8192, max j+i=8190, okay. Good.

But note if weights length W can be less than full (we may truncate)? We'll use full. If W not full, output may wrap into higher j? But we use full.

Sanitize:
```
def _sanitize(a):
    if a is None: return None
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12: return None
    a=a/s
    if a.max()>1000.0: # after normalization maybe if n small and one element large? But capped before so no
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s
    # enforce sum >0.01? for internal, yes sum1.
    return a
```
Since `max(0,x)` in evaluator and cap; after normalization sum1 no cap issue.

`_score(c,n)`: return 2.0*n*max(c). c may negative tiny; max(0).

```

Active weights:
```
def _active_weights(d, beta, mode, rng=None):
    n=d.size
    if n ==0: return np.array([])
    d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
    maxd=float(np.max(d))
    if maxd <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    epsd = maxd*1e-12
    # soft
    if beta <= 1e-6:
       soft = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       shifted = d - maxd
       e = np.exp(beta*shifted)
       s = e.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: soft = np.ones(n)/n
       else: soft=e/s
    # hard top
    near = int(np.sum(d >= maxd*0.995))  # plateau
    top_b = max(8, min(512, near*2 + 16))
    # For diversity maybe choose top_b based on mode? If rng, top_b random within? Keep deterministic.
    if top_b >= n:
       hard = np.ones(n)/n
    elif top_b <=1:
       idx=int(np.argmax(d)); hard=zeros; hard[idx]=1
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(d, -top_b)[-top_b:]
       hard=np.zeros(n); hard[idx]=1.0/top_b
    if mode == 0:
       return soft
    if mode == 1:
       return hard
    if mode == 2:
       return 0.5*soft + 0.5*hard
    if mode == 3:
       # soft with beta boosted?
       return soft
    return soft
```
Maybe near threshold 0.99 after max(d)=~0.5-1. Differences small. Use 0.98 to include plateau. top_b cap. If `near` includes many (maybe 1000), top_b=512. Good. Soft beta schedule. For hard mode, mode=1 use hard only. If maxd large, hard.

Could also use weights `∝ (d - maxd + maxd*0.05)` for top? Maybe not.

MMD line search details in code:
```
def _mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=1500.0, alpha_init=0.35, verbose=False, name=""):
    p=_sanitize(p0); if None return None,inf
    n=p.size; floor=1e-14/float(n)
    p=np.maximum(p,floor); p=p/p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_arr(p)
    cur_score=best_score
    c=_conv_self(p); d=n*c
    beta=float(beta_start); alpha=float(alpha_init)
    stall=0; it=0; last_print=...
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # mode selection
       if it < 10: mode=0
       elif it % 4 ==0: mode=1
       elif it % 4 ==1: mode=2
       else: mode=0
       weights=_active_weights(d,beta,mode)
       h=_valid_grad(weights,p)
       h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
       h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       maxh=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0
       if maxh <=1e-15:
          # perturb
       else:
          h=h/maxh
          # candidate alphas
          mults=(0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0)
          best_q=None; best_s=cur_score; best_a=alpha
          for m in mults:
             a=alpha*m
             if a<1e-5 or a>2.5: continue
             q=p*np.exp(-a*h)
             q=np.maximum(q,floor); s=q.sum(); q=q/s
             sc=_score_arr(q)
             # optional require sc < cur_score - 1e-12
             if sc < best_s:
                best_s=sc; best_q=q; best_a=a
          if best_q is not None and best_s < cur_score*(1-1e-12):
             p=best_q; cur_score=best_s; alpha=min(2.5, max(0.01, best_a)); stall=0
             if cur_score<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score; print...
          else:
             stall+=1; alpha=max(0.01, alpha*0.7)
          # drift recovery
          if cur_score > best_score*1.03 and (stall>0 or rng.random()<0.1):
             # try stepping back? reset to best if no recent improvement
             if stall>=8:
                p=best.copy(); c=_conv_self(p); d=n*c; cur_score=best_score; alpha=alpha_init*0.5; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.7); stall=0
       # perturb if stall...
       if stall>=20:
          std=0.05 if stall<80 else min(0.3, 0.05+0.002*(stall-80))
          q=_sanitize(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,std,n)))
          if q is not None:
             q=np.maximum(q,floor); q=q/q.sum()
             sc=_score_arr(q)
             if sc < best_score*1.02:
                p=q; cur_score=sc; stall=0
                if sc<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=sc
             else:
                p=best.copy(); c=_conv_self(p); d=n*c; cur_score=best_score
          else: reset
       # beta update each 10
       if it % 10==0: beta=min(beta_end, beta*1.12)
       it+=1
       # optional exact refresh d (did via score; but best_score recomputed c not stored). We need c/d for weights. If we updated p, we computed score but not d. We can compute c at top of loop. Restructure compute at top every iter.
    return best,best_score
```
Better: At start of each loop compute `c=_conv_self(p); d=n*c; cur_score=2*n*maxd` (unless just computed). For line search, candidate scores. At accept, we don't have candidate c but can compute c for accepted after? We can recompute at top next iter. To save one convolution, after accept we could store c from scoring? Our `_score_arr` recomputes. We can make `_score_c(q)` returns score and c. But each line we need only score. At accept, next loop compute c anyway. Fine.

If stall and reset to best, need recompute c/d. Simpler: top loop recomputes once; don't need c outside. In reset, just set p=best, then continue to top loop (but while loop body continues to beta increment; no issue, next iteration recompute). But if we `continue` after reset, skip updates. Simpler.

Potential issue: `_active_weights(d,beta,mode)` uses d from current p. If p reset in stall, d old; better to `continue` to next iteration after reset to recompute. In perturbation acceptance, could `continue`. Simpler:
```
while time...:
   # compute c,d,cur_score
   # compute h, line search
   if accept: update p; it+=1; continue
   else stall... if reset: p=best; it+=1; continue
```
This recomputes each iteration.

Function `_local_active_search(p0, rng, time_limit)`:
- p=..., best.
- While time:
   c=conv(p); d=n*c; top_k_indices = argpartition(d, topB)[-topB]
   For a few iterations (e.g., 2):
      k=random top idx
      # choose i uniform valid for k: lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(n-1,k)
      i=random in [lo,hi]; j=k-i
      if i==j: factor options f in [0.5,2]; p_i scaled, sum normalize? If i=j, p_i*f changes sum; okay.
      else: options: scale i up/down and j inverse or both same.
         transformations:
           p[i]*=f
           p[j]/=f  # preserve roughly
           p[i]*=f; p[j]*=f
           p[i]*=1/f; p[j]*=1/f
         f in [0.5,0.8,1.25,2]
      Evaluate exact; accept if lower.
   If no improve, perturb.
But many FFTs. Maybe too complex. Block/local enough.

**Zero insertion**: Inserting zeros between nonzero can shift cross terms and reduce central peak? If we generate a good length L and upsample with zeros (decimation), sum decreases? Normalization. Score effect? Zero padding doubles n but nonzero pattern length m. For full conv of zero-padded, coefficients have boundary effects; score maybe? If p = base repeated with zeros? `_initial_sequence(n, rng, mode, parent=None)`:

Implementation must be top-level, no closures.
```
def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=0, parent=None):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    arr=None
    m = mode % 9
    if m==0: # two-block random
       idx=int(rng.integers(1,n)); ratio=float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0)); p=np.ones(n); p[idx:]=ratio
    elif m==1: # random Fourier
       K=int(rng.integers(2,6)); p=np.ones(n); base=1.0
       for idx in range(K):
          f=float(rng.uniform(0.3,12.0))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2π))
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)) * (1.0/(1+idx*0.5))
          p = p + amp*np.cos(2π*f*np.arange(n)+ph)
       # maybe p=base + sum; but above starts p=1 plus
    elif m==2: # spline
       K=int(rng.integers(4,12)); knots=sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K)); heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K); p=interp+eps
    elif m==3: # blocks
       K=int(rng.integers(3,16)); edges=sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); blocks ...; p=zeros; heights lognormal; fill.
    elif m==4: # chirp
       idxs=np.arange(n); a=float(rng.uniform(-8,8)); b=float(rng.uniform(1,32)); ph=...; amp=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0); p=1+amp*cos(2π*(a*idxs + b*idxs*idxs/n)+ph) or b*idxs/n^2?
    elif m==5: # exp cosine
       f=rng.uniform(0.5,15); ph; amp=rng.uniform(0.5,2.0); p=np.exp(amp*cos(2π*f*arange+ph))
    elif m==6: # random permutation smoothed
       p=rng.permutation(n).astype(float)/max(1,n-1); p += rng.random(n)*0.0; p = _smooth_array(p, float(rng.uniform(2,8)))
    elif m==7: # Fejer-like positive:
       f=rng.uniform(0.5,10); ph; amp=rng.uniform(0.3,1); p=(1 - amp*cos(2π*f*arange+ph))**2? This nonnegative.
    elif m==8: # parent noise/resample
       if parent: p=resample(parent,n); if p: p=p*exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); p=smooth(samples=1)
       else p=ones
    else: p=ones
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float); p=np.where(finite,p,0); p=np.maximum(p,1e-9); p=p/p.sum()
    return p
```

Function `_fixed_direction_mpd(f, q0, rng, time_limit)`:

   weights=_active_weights(d,beta,mode)
   h=_valid_grad(weights,f)  # gradient wrt q is f_{k-j}, not p? Yes f fixed.
   normalize h.

   accept if maxz reduced
   beta up, stall...
return q,bestmax
```

Let's design fixed-direction with precomputed transforms for efficiency:
- Need full conv f*q. length 2n-1. Choose `L_full = next_pow2(2*n-1)`. `Ffull = rfft(f, L_full)`. For a q, `c = irfft(Ffull * rfft(q,L_full), L_full)[:2n-1]`. This is correct? For convolution f*q: yes.
- Need gradient h_j = ∑ weights[k] f[k-j], with weights length 2n-1. We can use L_grad = next_pow2(2*n) as before. Precompute `Fgrad = rfft(f, L_grad)`. For weights, compute `Wg = rfft(weights, L_grad)`, h=irfft(Wg * conj(Fgrad), L_grad)[:n]. Wait `_valid_grad` uses rfft(p) and weights with conj(p). Here p=f. So yes.
- In function, compute c and maxz. weights. h normalize.
- For candidate q: `q_new = q * exp(-alpha*h)` + tiny, normalize. Compute c using cached Ffull. Exact maxz.
This is efficient.

Pseudo code more precise:
```
def _mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=12, beta_end=900, alpha_init=0.3, max_alpha=2.0, verbose=False, tag=""):
    p=_sanitize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=p.size
    if n < 2: return p, _score_arr(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,time_limit)
    floor=1e-16/float(n)
    p=np.maximum(p,floor)
    p=p/(p.sum())
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_arr(p)
    beta=float(beta_start); alpha=float(alpha_init); stall=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_conv_self(p)
       c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0); c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       cur_score=2.0*n*float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf
       d=n*c
       if it<12: mode=0
       else:
          cycle=[0,1,1,2,0,2,1,0]
          mode=cycle[it%8]
       weights=_active_weights(d,beta,mode)
       h=_valid_grad(weights,p)
       h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0); h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       maxh=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
       if maxh <=1e-15:
          p_try=_sanitize(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)))
          if p_try is not None:
             p_try=np.maximum(p_try,floor); p_try=p_try+(1e-15); p_try=p_try/p_try.sum()
             sc=_score_arr(p_try)
             if sc < best_score: best=p_try; best_score=sc
          break? Or continue with stall.
       else:
          h=h/maxh
          mode_scale = 1.5 if mode==1 else (1.2 if mode==2 else 1.0)
          tested=[]
          for mult in (0.2,0.45,0.8,1.4,2.5):
             a=alpha*mult*mode_scale
             if a < 1e-4: a=1e-4
             if a > max_alpha: a=max_alpha
             if not tested or abs(a-tested[-1])>1e-4: tested.append(a)
          best_q=None; best_s=cur_score; best_a=alpha
          for a in tested[:5]:
             q=p*np.exp(-a*h) + floor
             s=q.sum()
             if s<=0 or not finite: continue
             q=q/s
             # maybe if max q >1000 impossible
             sc=_score_arr(q)
             if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                 best_s=sc; best_q=q; best_a=a
          if best_q is not None:
             p=best_q; cur_score=best_s
             # Update alpha: if best_a close to max*mult? use best_a / mode_scale / average mult? simpler alpha=best_a*1.1 capped
             alpha=min(max_alpha, max(1e-4, best_a*0.9))
             stall=0
             if cur_score < best_score:
                 best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
                 if verbose: print(...)
          else:
             stall+=1
             alpha=max(1e-4, alpha*0.7)
             if stall>=6 and cur_score > best_score*1.02:
                 p=best.copy(); alpha=alpha_init*0.5; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.6); stall=0
                 continue
       # perturb if stall high
       if stall >= 25:
          std=0.05 if stall<100 else min(0.35,0.05+0.002*(stall-100))
          p_try=_sanitize(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,std,n)) + floor)
          if p_try is not None:
             sc=_score_arr(p_try)
             if sc < best_score*1.03:
                 p=p_try; stall=0
                 if sc<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=sc
             else:
                 p=best.copy(); stall=0
          else:
             p=best.copy(); stall=0
       # inflate/recover if p max too high? no.
       if it % 7 ==0:
          beta=min(beta_end, beta*1.10)
       it+=1
       if it % 5000 ==0 and verbose: print
    return best, best_score
```

def _score_norm(p):
    if p is None: return inf
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return inf
    c=_conv_self(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    if mc<0 or not finite: return inf
    return 2.0*n*mc
```

Now, `_fixed_direction_mpd` implementation:
```
def _fixed_direction_mpd(f, q0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=15,beta_end=1000,alpha_init=0.5,verbose=False):
    f=_sanitize(f); if None return None,inf
    n=f.size; if n<2 return f,_score_norm(f)
    Lfull=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Ffull=np.fft.rfft(f,Lfull)
    Lgrad=_next_pow2(2*n); Fgrad=np.fft.rfft(f,Lgrad)
    q=_sanitize(q0); if None: q=np.ones(n)/n
    best=q.copy(); bestmax=... (conv f,q)
    beta... alpha... stall...
    while time:
       fz = np.fft.rfft(q,Lfull)
       z = irfft(Ffull*fz,Lfull)[:2*n-1]; z=np.maximum(z,0)
       maxz=float(np.max(z)); d=z/maxz if maxz>0 else zeros
       mode cycle as mpd
       weights=_active_weights(d,beta,mode)
       Wg=... rfft(weights,Lgrad); h=irfft(Wg*np.conj(Fgrad),Lgrad)[:n]; h=max(0)
       normalize
       alphas...
       for a: qn = q*exp(-a*h)+floor; normalize; z=... max; accept if maxz lower.
       update best
       beta/stall/reset
    return best_q,bestmax
```

Now, local block search with fast score.
```
def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit, tag=""):
  p=_sanitize(p0); if None ...
  n=p.size; floor=...
  best=p; best_score=_score_norm(p); cur_score=best_score
  factors=(0.45,0.75,1.2222,1.7778,?)
  deadline...
  stagn=0
  while time:
     r=random
     choose w
     if r<0.2: w=1
     elif r<0.6: w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//256)))
     elif r<0.9: w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//256), max(3,n//32)))
     else: w=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//32), min(n, max(4,n//4))))
     w=min(n,max(1,w)); start=rng.integers(0,n-w+1)
     base=p
     cur_best=base; cur_sc=cur_score
     for f in factors:
        q=base.copy(); q[start:start+w]=f
        q=np.maximum(q,floor); q=q/q.sum()
        sc=_score_norm(q)
        if sc<cur_sc: cur_sc=sc; cur_best=q
     if cur_best is not base:
        p=cur_best; cur_score=cur_sc; stagn=0; update best
     else:
        stagn+=1
        if stagn%10==0: random noise; if improves accept else reset
        if stagn>300: reset
  return best, best_score
```
Factors: Multiplying by 1 does nothing; exclude. Use (0.4,0.7,1.3,2.2) maybe 4. Could include 0.9/1.1 near for fine: (0.5,0.8,1.25,2). Use 4.
For w=1, scaling a single coordinate large can improve. Good.

Potential issue: Copying vector for each factor O(n) 4* length; okay. Could modify in place and restore. Simpler copy.

`_improve_candidate`:
```
def _improve_candidate(p0, n, rng, time_limit, tag):
    p = _resample(p0, n)
    if p is None: p=_initial_sequence(n,rng,1)
    t1 = 0.75*time_limit
    p1,s1 = _mpd(p, rng, t1, verbose=True, tag=tag)
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start)
    if rem > 10 and s1 finite:

def _score_from_rfft(comp, L, n):
   if L<=0 or n<=0: return inf
   out=np.fft.irfft(comp*comp, L)
   end=min(2*n-1, out.size)

   return float(2.0*n*mc)

Now, implement `_mpd` with cached transforms.

Detailed code for `_mpd`:
```
def _mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=12.0, beta_end=900.0, alpha_init=0.35, max_alpha=2.2, verbose=False, tag=""):
    p = _sanitize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n = p.size
    if time_limit <= 0 or n <= 1:
        return p, _score_norm(p)
    L = _next_pow2(2*n)
    floor = 1e-16 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor); p = p / p.sum()
    best = p.copy(); best_score = _score_norm(p)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    beta = float(beta_start); alpha = float(alpha_init); stall=0; iters=0
    mode_cycle=(0,1,1,2,0,2,1,0)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        cc = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, L)
        end = 2*n-1
        if cc.size < end: cc = cc[:cc.size]
        else: cc = cc[:end]
        cc = np.where(np.isfinite(cc), cc, 0.0)
        cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
        cur_score = float(2.0*n*np.max(cc)) if cc.size else inf
        d = n*cc
        if iters < 12: mode=0
        else: mode=mode_cycle[iters % len(mode_cycle)]
        weights = _active_weights(d, beta, mode)
        wf = np.fft.rfft(weights, L)
        hh = np.fft.irfft(wf * np.conjugate(fp), L)
        hh = hh[:n]
        hh = np.where(np.isfinite(hh), hh, 0.0); hh = np.maximum(hh, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(hh)) if hh.size else 0.0
        if maxh <= 1e-14:
            stall += 1
        else:
            hh = hh / maxh
            scale = 1.5 if mode == 1 else (1.2 if mode == 2 else 1.0)
            raw = (0.45,0.75,1.0,1.6,2.6)
            tested=[]
            for mult in raw:
                a = alpha * mult * scale
                if a < 1e-5: a=1e-5
                if a > max_alpha: a=max_alpha
                if not tested or abs(a - tested[-1]) > 1e-4:
                    tested.append(float(a))
            best_q = None; best_s = cur_score; best_a = alpha
            for a in tested:
                q = p * np.exp(-a * hh) + floor
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): continue
                q = q / s
                cq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
                sc = _score_from_rfft(cq, L, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-10:
                    best_s = sc; best_q = q; best_a = a
            if best_q is not None:
                p = best_q; cur_score = best_s
                alpha = float(np.clip(best_a * 0.9, 1e-4, max_alpha))
                stall = 0
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy(); best_score = cur_score
                    if verbose and (iters % 200 ==0): print(...)
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-4, alpha * 0.72)
                if stall >= 10 and cur_score > best_score * 1.03:
                    p = best.copy()
                    alpha = alpha_init * 0.5
                    beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.6)
                    stall = 0
        if stall >= 25:
            std = 0.06 if stall < 100 else min(0.35, 0.06 + 0.002 * (stall - 100))
            p_try = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, n)) + floor
            s = float(p_try.sum())
            if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
                p_try /= s
                sc = _score_norm(p_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                    p = p_try; stall = 0
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = p_try.copy(); best_score = sc
                else:
                    p = best.copy(); stall = 0
            else:
                p = best.copy(); stall = 0
        if iters % 6 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_end, beta * 1.09)
        iters += 1
    # final ensure best_score
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score=_score_norm(best)
    return best, best_score
```
Potential issue: `np.clip` returns numpy scalar; float() okay. `print` tag maybe length. To avoid too many prints, use `iters % 200`.

Should we recompute `best_score` after p? yes.

In `_score_from_rfft`, if output has small negative max? no.

`floor` addition ~1e-16/n very tiny; maybe too tiny to allow zeros. Use `floor = 1e-12 / float(n)`. Sum floor =1e-12, negligible. Good.

In line search, q = p*exp(...)+floor; normalize. If floor too small, zeros maybe recover. Use `1e-10/n`? Sum 1e-10. okay. Values min 1e-10/n, for n4096 ~2.4e-14. Still tiny. `1e-8/n` sum1e-8; might bias but allows recovery. Use `floor=1e-12/n` for numerical, but zero recovery maybe needs 1e-10? Multiplicative from floor very small, but if not in peaks, it can grow by normalization relative? If all others decrease, floor mass fraction can increase. Since floor sum tiny, if all mass concentrated elsewhere and floor mass grows relative, it may take many iterations. Better add small uniform mix in update: `q = (1 - 1e-7)*q + 1e-7*uniform`. This ensures mass 1e-7 in all coords. But adding uniform may worsen objectives by 1e-7. Accept if lower. Let's include a small `yellow = p * np.exp(-a*hh); y = 1e-6; q = (1-y)*yellow + y*(1/n); normalize`. This can increase zeros and regularize. The uniform baseline may slightly increase max but negligible. Use y=1e-5? too large. Use 1e-6. In line search, this adds O(n) operation. Good. It prevents collapse and allows evolution. After normalize. For best p, if y regularized, p has no exact zeros. We'll use `reg = 1e-6`.

Then floor not needed but keep.

Candidate q:
```
raw = p * np.exp(-a*hh)
q = (1.0-reg)*raw + reg*inv_n
s=q.sum(); q=q/s
```
`inv_n = 1.0/float(n)` precomputed.

This may add a tiny uniform component each accepted update; after many steps, p could converge to uniform? But normalization and relative decreases persist? The additive uniform each step in log-space approximates adding small constant; if repeated, it can raise valleys by y*inv_n / current mass. If current mass varies widely, it may flatten. Could be beneficial. y=1e-6 small. We'll include maybe for MPD only? In fixed direction, yes.

Now, `_fixed_direction_mpd` with reg:
```
def _fixed_direction_mpd(f, q0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=15, beta_end=1000, alpha_init=0.7, max_alpha=2.0):
   f=_sanitize(f); n=f.size; L=_next_pow2(2*n); inv_n=1/n; F=np.fft.rfft(f,L); floor=1e-12/n; q=_sanitize or uniform
   bestq=q; bestmax=...
   deadline...
   while time:
      fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L); z=irfft(F*fq,L); z[:out_len]; maxz; d=z/maxz; weights; wf; h=irfft(wf*conj(F),L)[:n]
      line search qn=(1-reg)*q*exp(-a*h_norm)+reg*inv_n; compute maxz; accept if maxz < bestmax - 1e-12
      stall/perturb/reset
      beta update
   return bestq,bestmax
```
But q0 if uniform, bestmax maybe average ~? f*q max. Good.

`_fixed_direction_update(f, rng, time_limit)`:
```
def _fixed_direction_update(f, rng, time_limit, tag=""):
    f0=_sanitize(f); if None return None,inf
    n=f0.size; score0=_score_norm(f0)
    rem=time_limit
    # run from uniform
    t1=min(rem, 0.5*time_limit)
    q,_ = _fixed_direction_mpd(f0, np.ones(n)/n, rng, t1...)
    best=f0; best_score=score0
    if q is not None:
       for t in (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.6):
           r=(1-t)*f0 + t*q; r=_sanitize(r); if r: sc=_score_norm(r); if sc<best: update
    # if remaining >, run from f0? Maybe if q improves, run again with q as q0?
    if time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start) > 20:
       q2,_ = _fixed_direction_mpd(best if improved else f0, uniform..., remaining/2)
       blends...
    return best
```
But running fixed direction for 200s may be heavy; but one. Use q0 uniform. Could also initialize q to current best p (which equals f?) That might not move? maybe start from `f0 * exp(random small)`.

Given time budget, we can call `_fixed_direction_update(best, rng, 120)` before local. It might improve. We'll include if rem>100.

Now, local search. Use `_score_norm` and direct `_conv_self` for peak targeting? Could specialized.
```
def _local_block_search(p0,rng,time_limit,verbose=False,tag=""):
  p=_sanitize; if time<=0 return
  n=p.size; inv_n; deadline; best; best_score; cur_score; stall=0
  factors=(0.45,0.75,1.35,2.2)
  while time:
    r=rng.random()
    # choose w
    if r<0.25: w=1
    elif r<0.65: w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//256+1)))
    elif r<0.9: w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//256), max(3,n//32+1)))
    else: w=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//32), min(n, n//4+1)))
    w=max(1,min(n,w))
    if stall > 8 or rng.random()<0.25:
       # target peaks
       c=_conv_self(p); d=n*c; top_b=min(32, d.size); idx=np.argpartition(d, -top_b)[-top_b]; k=int(idx[int(rng.integers(0,top_b))])
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if hi>lo:
          i_rel=int(np.argmax(p[lo:hi+1])); target=lo+i_rel
       else: target=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       start=int(target - w//2); start=max(0,min(start,n-w))
    else:
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
    current_best=p; current_score=cur_score
    for factor in factors:
       q=p.copy(); q[start:start+w]*=factor
       s=q.sum(); q=q/s
       sc=_score_norm(q)
       if sc < current_score - 1e-10:
          current_score=sc; current_best=q
    if current_best is not p:
       p=current_best; cur_score=current_score; stall=0; if cur_score<best: update
    else:
       stall+=1
       if stall % 12 ==0:
          q=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)); q=q/q.sum(); sc=_score_norm(q); if sc<best*1.03 accept else reset
       if stall > 300: reset
  return best
```

def _conv_self(a):

`_sanitize`:
```
def _sanitize(a):
    if a is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    except Exception: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12: return None
    arr=arr/s
    if arr.size ==0: return None
    if arr.max() > 1000.0:
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000); s=float(arr.sum()); if s<=0: return None; arr=arr/s
    return arr
```
`_score_norm` uses sanitized? It assumes normalized finite. To be safe:
```
def _score_norm(p):
    p=_sanitize(p); if p is None: return inf
    n=p.size; c=_conv_self(p); if c.size==0: return inf
    mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    if not finite or mc<0: mc=0
    return float(2*n*mc)
```
This sanitizes every score call; in `_mpd`, candidate q already sanitized but `_score_norm` only used outside. In local, okay. For speed local could use `_score_norm` with sanitize overhead; maybe okay. We can make `_score_norm_raw(a)` that only clips finite. But not needed? local 8000 calls sanitize O(n)=32M? Actually 8000*4096=32M, okay. Clean.

In `_mpd`, we use `_score_from_rfft` no sanitize. For random perturb `_score_norm`. Good.

`_fixed_direction_update`:
```
def _fixed_direction_update(f, rng, time_limit, tag=""):
   f0=_sanitize(f); if None...
   n=f0.size; s0=_score_norm(f0); best=f0; bs=s0
   if time_limit <=5: return
   elapsed = 0
   q_start = np.ones(n)/n
   for _ in range(2):
      rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
      if rem < 15: break
      t_dir = min(rem*0.45, 120.0)
      if t_dir < 5: break
      q,_ = _fixed_direction_mpd(best, q_start, rng, t_dir, tag=tag)
      if q is not None:
         for t in (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.6,0.8):
             r=(1-t)*best + t*q
             r=_sanitize(r)
             if r is not None:
                sc=_score_norm(r)
                if sc < bs: bs=sc; best=r.copy()
      q_start = _sanitize(q_start * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.15,n)))
      if q_start is None: q_start=np.ones(n)/n
   return best,bs
```

Now, let's reason about correctness of gradient h in fixed direction: `h = ifft(W * conj(F))[:n]`. Standard: if A has length L, B length L, ifft(A * conj(B))[j] = ∑_i A_{i+j} B_i with circular. Here A=weights length 2n-1, B=f length n. Need h_j = ∑_i f_i weights[j+i]. weights index j+i up to 2n-2. L>=2n, no wrap. Works. For direct objective c=conv(p,p): derivative of ∑ w_k c_k wrt p_j = 2∑ w_k p_{k-j} = 2∑ w_{j+i}p_i. Same. Good.

Let's write `_initial_sequence` fully:
```
def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=0, parent=None):
    if n <= 0: return None
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    t = idx / float(n)
    m = int(mode) % 9
    p = None
    if m == 0:
       split = int(rng.integers(1, n)); ratio = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 4.0)); p=np.ones(n); p[split:]=ratio; p += 1e-12
    elif m == 1:
       K = int(rng.integers(2,6)); p=np.ones(n, dtype=float)
       for j in range(K):
          f=float(rng.uniform(0.3,12.0)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0))* (1.0/(1.0+0.4*j))
          p += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*idx + ph)
    elif m == 2:
       K=int(rng.integers(4,13)); knots=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K)); heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K); p=np.interp(t, knots, heights); p += 1e-10
    elif m == 3:
       K=int(rng.integers(3,16)); if K<=1 p=ones; else edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); blocks=[0]+list(edges)+[1]; heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K); p=zeros; for each fill.
    elif m == 4:
       a=float(rng.uniform(-8,8)); b=float(rng.uniform(1,32)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)); arg=2*np.pi*(a*idx + b*(idx*idx)/float(n) + ph); p=1+amp*np.cos(arg); p+=1e-10
    elif m == 5:
       p = rng.permutation(n).astype(float); p += 1e-8
    elif m == 6:
       f=float(rng.uniform(0.5,15)); ph=...; amp=float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)); p=np.exp(amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*idx+ph)); p+=1e-10
    elif m == 7:
       f=float(rng.uniform(0.5,10)); ph=...; amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)); p=(1.0 - amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*idx+ph))**2; p+=1e-10
    else:
       if parent is not None:
          p=_resample(parent,n)
          if p is not None:
             p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.08)), n))
       else:
          p=np.ones(n)
          if n > 1:
             p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.1,n))
          p += 1e-12
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None: p=_sanitize(np.ones(n))
    return p
```
For mode3 block fill, ensure not use `blocks` list of floats; use loops. Fine.

`rng.permutation(n)` for n=4096 returns int64; fine.

def _periodic_candidate(n_final,L,rng,time_limit):
   pL = _initial_sequence(L, rng, int(rng.integers(0,9)))
   if pL is None: pL=np.ones(L)/L
   t=min(3.0, max(0.2, time_limit*0.15))
   pL,_ = _mpd(pL,rng,t,beta_start=10,beta_end=200,alpha_init=0.35)
   if pL is None: pL=np.ones(L)/L
   return _tile_to_length(pL,n_final)

def _generate_starts(n,rng,time_limit,parent,keep=8):
   cands=[]
   deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,time_limit)
   # parent variants
   if parent is not None:
      for mode in (8,): p=_initial_sequence(n,rng,mode,parent); p=_smooth_array(p, float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0))); add
      p=_resample(parent,n); add; noisy; add
   # two block
   tb=_two_block_best(n); add
   # few deterministic initial modes
   for mode in (1,4,5,7,0,6,2,3,8):
      p=_initial_sequence(n,rng,mode,parent)
      if p is not None:
         if mode in (0,2,3,5,7): p=_smooth_array(p, float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.5)))
         add(p)
   # periodic small
   periodic_time = min(20.0, max(4.0, 0.1*time_limit)) if time_limit>20 else 0
   for L in (32,64,128):
      if n % L ==0 and time.monotonic()<deadline-2:
         p=_periodic_candidate(n,L,rng,periodic_time/3.0)
         if p: add(p, keep=16)
   count=0; max_count=250 if n>1500 else 500
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0 and count < max_count:
      mode=int(rng.integers(0,9))
      p=_initial_sequence(n,rng,mode,parent)
      if p is not None:
         if mode in (0,2,3,5,7): p=_smooth_array(p, float(rng.uniform(0.3,3.0)))
         if time.monotonic() < deadline:
            sc=_score_norm(p); _add_candidate(cands,p,sc,16)
      count+=1
   cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
   return [cands[i][2].copy() for i in range(min(keep,len(cands)))]
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)**2

using multi-start multiplicative mirror descent on the simplex,
hard/soft active-set constraints, a linearized fixed-direction solver,
block local search, periodic short-period candidates, and optional
upsample polish.  The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, which
does not change the objective.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n <= 768:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return out[:2 * n - 1].astype(float, copy=False)


def _score_from_rfft(comp, L, n):
    if L <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    out = np.fft.irfft(comp * comp, L)
    end = min(2 * n - 1, out.size)
    if end <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    seg = out[:end]
    seg = np.where(np.isfinite(seg), seg, 0.0)
    mc = float(np.max(seg))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def _sanitize(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.max() > 1000.0:
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def _score_norm(p):
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_self(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def _smooth_array(p, samples):
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    s = float(samples)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0 or p.size < 5:
        return p
    rad = int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0 * s)))
    k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1, dtype=float)
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / s) ** 2)
    kern /= kern.sum()
    out = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    return _sanitize(out)


def _resample(a, m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    a = _sanitize(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0) + 1e-12
    return _sanitize(out)


def _tile_to_length(a, m):
    a = _sanitize(a)
    if a is None or m <= 0:
        return None
    L = int(a.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return None
    reps = int((int(m) + L - 1) // L)
    out = np.repeat(a, reps)[:m]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-12)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return out / s


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, score, keep=16):
    a = _sanitize(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score_norm(a)
    cands.append((float(score), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    if len(cands) > keep:
        cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
        del cands[-1]


def _get_best(cands):
    if not cands:
        return None, float("inf")
    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    return cands[0][2].copy(), float(cands[0][0])


def _get_parent_arr():
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    return _sanitize(raw)


def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=0, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    t = idx / float(n)
    m = int(mode) % 9
    p = None

    if m == 0:
        split = int(rng.integers(1, n))
        ratio = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 4.0))
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        p[split:] = ratio
        p += 1e-12

    elif m == 1:
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        for j in range(K):
            f = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 12.0))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) * (1.0 / (1.0 + 0.4 * j))
            p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx + ph)

    elif m == 2:
        K = int(rng.integers(4, 13))
        knots = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.interp(t, knots, heights)
        p += 1e-10

    elif m == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 16))
        if K <= 1:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        else:
            edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1))
            blocks = [0.0]
            blocks.extend(list(edges))
            blocks.append(1.0)
            heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
            p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for j in range(K):
                s0 = max(0, int(round(blocks[j] * n)))
                e0 = max(s0 + 1, int(round(blocks[j + 1] * n)))
                e0 = min(n, e0)
                p[s0:e0] = float(heights[j])
        p += 1e-12

    elif m == 4:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-8.0, 8.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 32.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        arg = 2.0 * np.pi * (a * idx + b * (idx * idx) / float(n) + ph)
        p = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(arg)
        p += 1e-10

    elif m == 5:
        p = rng.permutation(n).astype(float)
        p += 1e-8

    elif m == 6:
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 15.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        p = np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx + ph))
        p += 1e-10

    elif m == 7:
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 10.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        p = (1.0 - amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx + ph)) ** 2
        p += 1e-10

    else:
        if parent is not None:
            p = _resample(parent, n)
            if p is not None:
                p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.08)), n))
        else:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
            if n > 1:
                p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, n))
            p += 1e-12

    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        p = _sanitize(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
    return p


def _two_block_best(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 4:
        return None
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for frac in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80):
        idx = int(round(frac * n))
        if idx <= 0 or idx >= n:
            continue
        for ratio in (0.25, 0.50, 0.80, 1.25, 2.00, 4.00):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
            p[idx:] = ratio
            p = _sanitize(p)
            if p is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_norm(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best = p.copy()
    return best


def _periodic_candidate(n_final, L, rng, time_limit):
    L = int(L)
    if n_final <= 0 or L <= 0:
        return None
    pL = _initial_sequence(L, rng, int(rng.integers(0, 9)))
    if pL is None:
        pL = np.ones(L, dtype=float) / float(L)
    t = max(0.2, min(3.0, float(time_limit) * 0.25))
    pL, _ = _mpd(pL, rng, t, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=220.0, alpha_init=0.35)
    if pL is None:
        pL = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
    return _tile_to_length(pL, n_final)


def _generate_starts(n, rng, time_limit, parent, keep=8):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    cands = []
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    if parent is not None:
        pr = _resample(parent, n)
        if pr is not None:
            _add_candidate(cands, pr, None, 16)
            noisy = _sanitize(pr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)))
            if noisy is not None:
                noisy = _smooth_array(noisy, float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)))
                if noisy is not None:
                    _add_candidate(cands, noisy, None, 16)
        for mode in (8,):
            pp = _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode, parent)
            if pp is not None:
                pp = _smooth_array(pp, float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5)))
                if pp is not None:
                    _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)

    tb = _two_block_best(n)
    if tb is not None:
        _add_candidate(cands, tb, None, 16)

    for mode in (1, 4, 5, 7, 0, 6, 2, 3):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        pp = _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode, parent)
        if pp is not None:
            if mode in (0, 2, 3, 5, 7):
                pp2 = _smooth_array(pp, float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5)))
                if pp2 is not None:
                    pp = pp2
            _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)

    if time_limit > 90.0:
        per = max(0.4, min(1.5, (time_limit - 20.0) / 36.0))
        for L in (32, 64, 128, 256):
            if n % L != 0:
                continue
            for _ in range(4):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12.0:
                    break
                pp = _periodic_candidate(n, L, rng, per)
                if pp is not None:
                    _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)

    count = 0
    max_count = 250 if n > 1500 else 500
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0 and count < max_count:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 9))
        pp = _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode, parent)
        if pp is not None:
            if mode in (0, 2, 3, 5, 7):
                pp2 = _smooth_array(pp, float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0)))
                if pp2 is not None:
                    pp = pp2
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
                _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)
        count += 1

    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    out = []
    limit = min(keep, len(cands))
    for i in range(limit):
        out.append(cands[i][2].copy())
    return out


def _active_weights(d, beta, mode):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = int(d.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    maxd = float(np.max(d))
    if maxd <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)

    beta = float(max(0.0, beta))
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        soft = np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    else:
        shifted = d - maxd
        e = np.exp(beta * shifted)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            soft = np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
        else:
            soft = e / s

    plateau = int(np.sum(d >= maxd * 0.985))
    base_top = max(16, min(512, m // 32))
    top_b = max(base_top, min(512, plateau * 3))
    top_b = int(min(m, max(1, top_b)))

    if top_b >= m:
        hard = np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    elif top_b <= 1:
        hard = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        hard[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, -top_b)[-top_b:]
        hard = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        hard[idx] = 1.0 / float(top_b)

    mode = int(mode)
    if mode == 1:
        return hard
    if mode == 2:
        return 0.5 * soft + 0.5 * hard
    return soft


def _mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=12.0, beta_end=900.0,
         alpha_init=0.35, max_alpha=2.2, verbose=False, tag=""):
    p = _sanitize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    L = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)
    reg = 1e-6
    floor = 1e-14 / float(n)

    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None, float("inf")
    p = p / s

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(p)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    beta = float(beta_start)
    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    stall = 0
    iters = 0
    mode_cycle = (0, 1, 1, 2, 0, 2, 1, 0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        cc = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)
        end = 2 * n - 1
        if cc.size < end:
            cc = cc[:cc.size]
        else:
            cc = cc[:end]
        cc = np.where(np.isfinite(cc), cc, 0.0)
        cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(cc)) if cc.size else float("inf")
        d = float(n) * cc

        if iters < 12:
            mode = 0
        else:
            mode = mode_cycle[iters % len(mode_cycle)]

        weights = _active_weights(d, beta, mode)
        wf = np.fft.rfft(weights, L)
        hh = np.fft.irfft(wf * np.conjugate(fp), L)
        hh = hh[:n]
        hh = np.where(np.isfinite(hh), hh, 0.0)
        hh = np.maximum(hh, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(hh)) if hh.size else 0.0

        if maxh <= 1e-14:
            stall += 1
        else:
            hh = hh / maxh
            scale = 1.5 if mode == 1 else (1.2 if mode == 2 else 1.0)
            raw_a = (
                alpha * scale * 0.5,
                alpha * scale,
                alpha * scale * 2.0,
                0.05,
                0.18,
            )
            tested = []
            for aa in raw_a:
                aa = float(aa)
                if not np.isfinite(aa):
                    continue
                aa = min(max_alpha, max(1e-5, aa))
                if not tested or abs(aa - tested[-1]) > 1e-4:
                    tested.append(aa)

            if not tested:
                tested = [0.05]

            best_q = None
            best_s = cur_score
            best_a = alpha

            for aa in tested:
                raw = p * np.exp(-aa * hh)
                q = (1.0 - reg) * raw + reg * inv_n
                q = np.maximum(q, floor)
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                q = q / s
                cq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
                sc = _score_from_rfft(cq, L, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-10:
                    best_s = sc
                    best_q = q
                    best_a = aa

            if best_q is not None:
                p = best_q
                cur_score = best_s
                alpha = float(np.clip(best_a * 0.9, 1e-4, max_alpha))
                stall = 0
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                    if verbose and (iters % 500 == 0):
                        print(f"mpd {tag} it={iters} score={best_score:.7f}")
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-4, alpha * 0.72)
                if stall >= 10 and cur_score > best_score * 1.03:
                    p = best.copy()
                    alpha = max(1e-3, alpha_init * 0.5)
                    beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.6)
                    stall = 0

        if stall >= 25:
            std = 0.06 if stall < 100 else min(0.35, 0.06 + 0.002 * (stall - 100))
            p_try = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, n)) + floor
            s = float(p_try.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                p_try = p_try / s
                sc = _score_norm(p_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                    p = p_try
                    stall = 0
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = p_try.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                else:
                    p = best.copy()
                    stall = 0
            else:
                p = best.copy()
                stall = 0

        if iters % 6 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.09)

        iters += 1

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _score_norm(best)
    return best, best_score


def _fixed_direction_mpd(f, q0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=15.0, beta_end=1200.0,
                         alpha_init=0.70, max_alpha=2.2, verbose=False, tag=""):
    f = _sanitize(f)
    if f is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(f.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return f, _score_norm(f)

    L = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)
    reg = 1e-6
    floor = 1e-14 / float(n)

    q = _sanitize(q0)
    if q is None:
        q = np.full(n, inv_n, dtype=float)
    q = np.maximum(q, floor)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(n, inv_n, dtype=float)
    else:
        q = q / s

    F = np.fft.rfft(f, L)

    def _max_from_q(zz):
        zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, 0.0)
        zz = np.maximum(zz, 0.0)
        if zz.size < out_len:
            return 0.0
        return float(np.max(zz[:out_len]))

    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    z = np.fft.irfft(F * fq, L)
    curmax = _max_from_q(z)
    bestq = q.copy()
    bestmax = curmax

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    beta = float(beta_start)
    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    stall = 0
    iters = 0
    mode_cycle = (0, 1, 1, 2, 0, 2, 1, 0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        fq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
        z = np.fft.irfft(F * fq, L)
        curmax = _max_from_q(z)
        if curmax <= 1e-15:
            d = np.zeros(out_len, dtype=float)
        else:
            zz = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            zz = np.maximum(zz, 0.0)
            d = zz[:out_len] / curmax

        if iters < 10:
            mode = 0
        else:
            mode = mode_cycle[iters % len(mode_cycle)]

        weights = _active_weights(d, beta, mode)
        wf = np.fft.rfft(weights, L)
        hh = np.fft.irfft(wf * np.conjugate(F), L)
        hh = hh[:n]
        hh = np.where(np.isfinite(hh), hh, 0.0)
        hh = np.maximum(hh, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(hh)) if hh.size else 0.0

        if maxh <= 1e-14:
            stall += 1
        else:
            hh = hh / maxh
            scale = 1.4 if mode == 1 else (1.15 if mode == 2 else 1.0)
            raw_a = (
                alpha * scale * 0.5,
                alpha * scale,
                alpha * scale * 2.0,
                0.07,
                0.22,
            )
            tested = []
            for aa in raw_a:
                aa = float(aa)
                if not np.isfinite(aa):
                    continue
                aa = min(max_alpha, max(1e-5, aa))
                if not tested or abs(aa - tested[-1]) > 1e-4:
                    tested.append(aa)
            if not tested:
                tested = [0.1]

            best_q = None
            best_m = curmax
            for aa in tested:
                raw = q * np.exp(-aa * hh)
                qn = (1.0 - reg) * raw + reg * inv_n
                qn = np.maximum(qn, floor)
                s = float(qn.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                qn = qn / s
                fqn = np.fft.rfft(qn, L)
                zn = np.fft.irfft(F * fqn, L)
                mn = _max_from_q(zn)
                if np.isfinite(mn) and mn < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best_m = mn
                    best_q = qn

            if best_q is not None:
                q = best_q
                curmax = best_m
                alpha = min(max_alpha, max(1e-4, alpha * 1.15))
                stall = 0
                if curmax < bestmax:
                    bestq = q.copy()
                    bestmax = curmax
                    if verbose and (iters % 400 == 0):
                        print(f"fixed {tag} it={iters} maxdir={bestmax:.7f}")
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-4, alpha * 0.72)
                if stall >= 12 and curmax > bestmax * 1.05:
                    q = bestq.copy()
                    alpha = max(1e-3, alpha_init * 0.5)
                    beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.6)
                    stall = 0

        if stall >= 25:
            std = 0.07 if stall < 100 else min(0.35, 0.07 + 0.002 * (stall - 100))
            q_try = q * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, n)) + floor
            s = float(q_try.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                q_try = q_try / s
                fqtry = np.fft.rfft(q_try, L)
                ztry = np.fft.irfft(F * fqtry, L)
                mtry = _max_from_q(ztry)
                if np.isfinite(mtry) and mtry <= bestmax * 1.06:
                    q = q_try
                    stall = 0
                    if mtry < bestmax:
                        bestq = q.copy()
                        bestmax = mtry
                else:
                    q = bestq.copy()
                    stall = 0
            else:
                q = bestq.copy()
                stall = 0

        if iters % 6 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.10)

        iters += 1

    return bestq, bestmax


def _fixed_direction_update(f, rng, time_limit, tag=""):
    f0 = _sanitize(f)
    if f0 is None or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return f0, (float("inf") if f0 is None else _score_norm(f0))
    n = int(f0.size)
    best = f0.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    q_start = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)

    for _ in range(2):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        rem = float(time_limit) - elapsed
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        tdir = min(rem * 0.45, 130.0)
        if tdir < 5.0:
            break
        q, _ = _fixed_direction_mpd(best, q_start, rng, tdir, tag=tag)
        if q is not None:
            for blend in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80):
                r = (1.0 - blend) * best + blend * q
                r = _sanitize(r)
                if r is not None:
                    sc = _score_norm(r)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best = r.copy()
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if float(time_limit) - elapsed < 15.0:
            break
        q_start = _sanitize(q_start * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n)))
        if q_start is None:
            q_start = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)

    return best, best_score


def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit, tag=""):
    p = _sanitize(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return p, (float("inf") if p is None else _score_norm(p))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(p)
    cur_score = best_score
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    stall = 0
    factors = (0.45, 0.75, 1.35, 2.20)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.25:
            w = 1
        elif r < 0.65:
            hi = max(2, n // 256)
            w = int(rng.integers(1, hi + 1))
        elif r < 0.90:
            lo = max(2, n // 256)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 32)
            w = int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi) + 1))
        else:
            lo = max(3, n // 32)
            hi = max(lo + 1, min(n, n // 4))
            w = int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi) + 1))

        w = int(min(max(1, w), n))

        if stall > 8 or float(rng.random()) < 0.25:
            c = _conv_self(p)
            d = float(n) * c
            d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
            d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
            top_b = min(32, max(1, int(d.size)))
            if top_b <= 1:
                k = int(np.argmax(d)) if d.size else 0
            elif top_b >= d.size:
                k = int(np.argmax(d))
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(d, -top_b)[-top_b:]
                k = int(idx[int(rng.integers(0, len(idx)))])
            lo_i = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi_i = min(n - 1, k)
            if hi_i < lo_i:
                target = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            else:
                target = lo_i + int(np.argmax(p[lo_i:hi_i + 1]))
            start = int(target - w // 2)
            start = max(0, min(start, n - w))
        else:
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        current_best = p
        current_score = cur_score

        for factor in factors:
            q = p.copy()
            q[start:start + w] *= float(factor)
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            s = float(q.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            q = q / s
            sc = _score_norm(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score - 1e-10:
                current_score = sc
                current_best = q

        if current_best is not p or current_score < cur_score:
            p = current_best
            cur_score = current_score
            stall = 0
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
                if tag:
                    print(f"local {tag} score={best_score:.7f}")
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 12 == 0:
                q = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    q = q / s
                    sc = _score_norm(q)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.03:
                        p = q
                        cur_score = sc
                        stall = 0
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best = q.copy()
                            best_score = sc
                    else:
                        p = best.copy()
                        cur_score = best_score
                        stall = 0
                else:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    stall = 0
            if stall > 300:
                p = best.copy()
                cur_score = best_score
                stall = 0

    return best, best_score


def _improve_candidate(p0, n, rng, time_limit, tag="", jitter=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    p = _resample(p0, n)
    if p is None:
        p = _initial_sequence(n, rng, 1)
    if p is None:
        p = _sanitize(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    if jitter > 0.0:
        p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(jitter), n))
        p = _sanitize(p)
        if p is None:
            p = _sanitize(np.ones(n, dtype=float))

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(p)

    t1 = max(5.0, 0.75 * float(time_limit))
    p1, s1 = _mpd(p, rng, min(t1, float(time_limit) - 5.0),
                  beta_start=10.0, beta_end=1000.0, alpha_init=0.35,
                  verbose=False, tag=tag)
    if p1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
        best, best_score = p1, s1

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = float(time_limit) - elapsed
    tloc = min(0.20 * float(time_limit), max(0.0, rem - 10.0))
    if tloc > 10.0:
        p2, s2 = _local_block_search(best, rng, tloc, tag=tag)
        if p2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best, best_score = p2, s2

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = float(time_limit) - elapsed
    t2 = max(0.0, rem - 5.0)
    if t2 > 10.0:
        p3, s3 = _mpd(best, rng, t2, beta_start=50.0, beta_end=1800.0,
                      alpha_init=0.22, verbose=False, tag=tag)
        if p3 is not None and np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
            best, best_score = p3, s3

    return best, best_score


def _official_score(arr):
    a = _sanitize(arr)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return _score_norm(a)


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        budget = 120.0
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 30.0)

    try:
        iseed = int(seed) % 1000000007
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng(iseed + 1)

    cands = []
    parent = _get_parent_arr()
    if parent is not None:
        _add_candidate(cands, parent, None, 16)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 1200.0:
        main_n = 4096
    elif rem > 600.0:
        main_n = 3072
    else:
        main_n = 2048

    starts = []
    if rem > 120.0:
        gen_time = min(140.0, max(10.0, rem - 160.0))
        if time_limit_used := gen_time:
            pass
        starts = _generate_starts(main_n, rng, gen_time, parent, keep=10)
        for pp in starts:
            _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)
    else:
        pp = _resample(parent, main_n) if parent is not None else None
        if pp is None:
            pp = _initial_sequence(main_n, rng, 1)
        starts = [pp]
        _add_candidate(cands, pp, None, 16)

    best_arr, best_score = _get_best(cands)
    if best_arr is not None:
        print(f"init n={main_n} best={best_score:.7f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.2f}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 350.0:
        p1_time = min(0.50 * rem, max(250.0, rem - 650.0))
        fracs = (0.30, 0.25, 0.20, 0.12, 0.07, 0.05, 0.04, 0.03)
        starts_sorted = sorted(starts, key=lambda x: _score_norm(x))
        for i, pp in enumerate(starts_sorted[:len(fracs)]):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 70.0:
                break
            alloc = fracs[i] * p1_time
            alloc = min(alloc, max(0.0, rem - 70.0))
            if alloc < 25.0:
                continue
            pp2, s2 = _improve_candidate(pp, main_n, rng, alloc,
                                         tag=f"A{i}", jitter=0.02)
            if pp2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2):
                _add_candidate(cands, pp2, s2, 16)
        best_arr, best_score = _get_best(cands)
        if best_arr is not None:
            print(f"phaseA best={best_score:.7f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.2f}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 300.0:
        cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
        top = []
        seen = set()
        for item in cands:
            arr = item[2]
            key = arr.tobytes()
            if key not in seen and arr is not None:
                seen.add(key)
                top.append(arr.copy())
            if len(top) >= 3:
                break
        p2_time = min(0.45 * rem, max(180.0, rem - 350.0))
        fracs = (0.45, 0.35, 0.20)
        for i, pp in enumerate(top[:len(fracs)]):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 70.0:
                break
            alloc = fracs[i] * p2_time
            alloc = min(alloc, max(0.0, rem - 70.0))
            if alloc < 35.0:
                continue
            pp2, s2 = _improve_candidate(pp, main_n, rng, alloc,
                                         tag=f"B{i}", jitter=0.008)
            if pp2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2):
                _add_candidate(cands, pp2, s2, 16)
        best_arr, best_score = _get_best(cands)
        if best_arr is not None:
            print(f"phaseB best={best_score:.7f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.2f}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_arr is not None and rem > 160.0:
        t_fd = min(180.0, 0.55 * rem)
        fp, fs = _fixed_direction_update(best_arr, rng, t_fd, tag="FD")
        if fp is not None and np.isfinite(fs):
            _add_candidate(cands, fp, fs, 16)

    best_arr, best_score = _get_best(cands)
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_arr is not None and rem > 90.0:
        t_loc = min(130.0, 0.55 * rem)
        lp, ls = _local_block_search(best_arr, rng, t_loc, tag="LOC")
        if lp is not None and np.isfinite(ls):
            _add_candidate(cands, lp, ls, 16)

    best_arr, best_score = _get_best(cands)
    if best_arr is not None:
        print(f"pre-up best={best_score:.7f} len={len(best_arr)} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.2f}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_arr is not None and rem > 450.0 and best_score > 1.50:
        if len(best_arr) <= 2048:
            big_n = 4096
        elif len(best_arr) <= 4096:
            big_n = 6144
        else:
            big_n = None
        if big_n is not None:
            big = _resample(best_arr, big_n)
            if big is not None:
                big = _sanitize(big * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, big_n)))
                alloc = min(420.0, max(0.0, rem - 80.0))
                bp, bs = _improve_candidate(big, big_n, rng, alloc,
                                            tag="BIG", jitter=0.02)
                if bp is not None and np.isfinite(bs):
                    _add_candidate(cands, bp, bs, 16)

    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    if cands:
        selected = cands[0][2]
        selected_score = float(cands[0][0])
    else:
        selected = _sanitize(np.ones(64, dtype=float))
        selected_score = _score_norm(selected)

    attempts = 0
    for item in cands[:10]:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 4.0:
            break
        attempts += 1
        if attempts > 6:
            break
        arr = item[2]
        if arr is None:
            continue
        sc = _official_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < selected_score:
            selected_score = sc
            selected = arr.copy()

    out = _sanitize(selected)
    if out is None:
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=float)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=float)
        s = 64.0
    out = out / s
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
